	local function fn50(arg, arg2)
		for _, v5 in Workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(arg, 0, arg2) do
			if v5.CanCollide and (v5:GetClosestPointOnSurface(arg) - arg).Magnitude <= 0 then
				return false
			end
		end

		return true
	end

	local function fn51(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		local lookVector = CFrame.lookAt(arg * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1), arg2 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).LookVector
		local tbl18 = {}

		for _, v5 in tbl15, nil, nil do
			if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
				table.insert(tbl18, arg + v5 * 6)
			end
		end

		for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
			if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
				table.insert(tbl18, arg + v5 * 6)
			end
		end

		for _, v5 in tbl16, nil, nil do
			if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
				table.insert(tbl18, arg + v5 * 12)
			end
		end

		for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
			if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
				table.insert(tbl18, arg + v5 * 12)
			end
		end

		for _, v5 in tbl18, nil, nil do
			if not Workspace:Raycast(arg2, v5 - arg2, arg4) and fn50(v5, arg5) then
				return v5
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	tbl13.Scan = function(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4, arg5)
		local lookVector = CFrame.lookAt(arg2 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1), arg3 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)).LookVector
		if tbl13.Cache[arg5] then
			return table.unpack(tbl13.Cache[arg5])
		end

		if arg4 then
			if (arg2 - arg4).Magnitude < 30 then
				local tbl18 = {}
				local tbl19 = {}

				if (arg2 - arg4).Magnitude < 7.5 then
					table.insert(tbl18, arg4)
				else
					table.insert(tbl18, arg4)
				end

				table.insert(tbl19, arg3)

				for _, v5 in Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems() do
					local vector = Vector3.fromNormalId(v5)

					if (vector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(-lookVector) > -0.5 then
						local n = arg3 + vector * 6

						if fn50(n, overlapParams) then
							table.insert(tbl19, n)
						end

						local n2 = arg3 + vector * 12

						if fn50(n2, overlapParams) then
							table.insert(tbl19, n2)
						end
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl15, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl18, arg2 + v5 * 6)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl18, arg2 + v5 * 6)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl16, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl18, arg2 + v5 * 12)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl18, arg2 + v5 * 12)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl19, nil, nil do
					for _, v6 in tbl18, nil, nil do
						if not Workspace:Raycast(v5, v6 - v5, raycastParams2) and fn50(v6, overlapParams) then
							tbl13.Cache[arg5] = { v6, v5 }
							return v6, v5
						end
					end
				end
			else
				local tbl18 = {}
				table.insert(tbl18, arg3)

				for _, v5 in Enum.NormalId:GetEnumItems() do
					local vector = Vector3.fromNormalId(v5)

					if (vector * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(-lookVector) > -0.5 then
						local n = arg3 + vector * 6

						if fn50(n, overlapParams) then
							table.insert(tbl18, n)
						end

						local n2 = arg3 + vector * 12

						if fn50(n2, overlapParams) then
							table.insert(tbl18, n2)
						end
					end
				end

				local tbl19 = {}

				for _, v5 in tbl15, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl19, arg2 + v5 * 6)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl19, arg2 + v5 * 6)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl16, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl19, arg2 + v5 * 12)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl17, nil, nil do
					if (v5 * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)):Dot(lookVector) > -0.5 then
						table.insert(tbl19, arg2 + v5 * 12)
					end
				end

				for _, v5 in tbl18, nil, nil do
					for _, v6 in tbl19, nil, nil do
						if not Workspace:Raycast(v5, v6 - v5, raycastParams2) and fn50(v6, overlapParams) then
							tbl13.Cache[arg5] = { v6, v5 }
							return v6, v5
						end
					end
				end
			end
		else
			local v5 = fn51(arg2, arg3, arg5, raycastParams2, overlapParams)
			if v5 then
				tbl13.Cache[arg5] = { v5 }
				return v5
			end
		end
	end

	local n2 = 64
	local tbl19 = { inner = {}, main = {}, outer = {} }

	for i = 1, 64 do

	local instance = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	instance.Name = "FOVGradient"
	instance.IgnoreGuiInset = true

	local function fn53()
		local v6 = instance3
		local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new
		local tbl20 = {}
		local v7 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, tbl12.fill_color_1)
		local v8 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, tbl12.fill_color_2)
		local new = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
		local fillColor3 = tbl12.fill_color_3
		tbl20[1] = v7
		tbl20[2] = v8

		do
			local values = table.pack(new(1, fillColor3))
			table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, tbl20)
		end

		v6.Color = colorSequence(tbl20)
	end

	fn53()

	local function fn54(arg, arg2, arg3, arg4)
		local n3 = arg4 % 1
		if n3 < 0.5 then
			return arg:Lerp(arg2, n3 * 2)
		end
		return arg2:Lerp(arg3, (n3 - 0.5) * 2)
	end

	local flag4 = false
	local tbl20 = nil

	local function fn55(arg)
		local position = currentCamera.CFrame.Position
		local raycastParams3 = RaycastParams.new()
		raycastParams3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
		raycastParams3.FilterDescendantsInstances = { localPlayer3.Character, currentCamera }
		local hit = Workspace:Raycast(position, arg.Position - position, raycastParams3)
		if not hit then
			return true
		end
		return hit.Instance:IsDescendantOf(arg.Parent)
	end

	local function fn56()
		local fovSize = tbl12.fov_size
		local vector2

		if flag3 then
			local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
			vector2 = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
		else
			vector2 = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		end

		local v6 = nil

		for _, v7 in Players:GetPlayers() do
			if v7 ~= localPlayer3 then
				if not (tbl12.team_check and v7.Team == localPlayer3.Team) then
					if tbl12.friend_check then
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return localPlayer3:IsFriendsWith(v7.UserId)
						end)

						if ok and result then
							continue
						end
					end

					local character = v7.Character

					if character then
						local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
						local v8 = character:FindFirstChild(tbl12.target_part)

						if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and v8 then
							local v9, v10 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(v8.Position)

							if v10 then
								local magnitude = (Vector2.new(v9.X, v9.Y) - vector2).Magnitude

								if magnitude < fovSize then
									if tbl12.wall_check and not tbl12.wallbang then
										if not fn55(v8) then
											continue
										end
									end

									v6 = v7
									fovSize = magnitude
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v6
	end

	local function fn57(arg, arg2)
		if tbl12.rotation_mode == "rotate to left" then
			return (arg + arg2 * 0.1) % 1
		end

		if tbl12.rotation_mode == "rotate to right" then
			return (arg - arg2 * 0.1) % 1
		end

		if tbl12.rotation_mode == "forward and backward" then
			return (math.sin(arg2 + arg * 3.1415926535897931 * 2) + 1) * 0.5
		end
		return arg
	end

	local flag5 = false

	RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
		local showFov = tbl12.show_fov
		local fillFov = tbl12.fill_fov

		if not showFov and not fillFov then
			if flag4 then
				for i = 1, 64 do
					tbl19.main[i].Visible = false
					tbl19.inner[i].Visible = false
					tbl19.outer[i].Visible = false
				end

				instance2.Visible = false
				flag4 = false
			end
		else
			flag4 = true
			local vector2

			if flag3 then
				local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
				vector2 = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
			else
				vector2 = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
			end

			local fovSize = tbl12.fov_size
			local fillTransparency = tbl12.fill_transparency
			local rotationSpeed = tbl12.rotation_speed
			local n3 = v2() * rotationSpeed
			local n4

			if tbl12.gradient_position_type == "north" then
				n4 = -1.5707963267948966
			elseif tbl12.gradient_position_type == "east" then
				n4 = 0
			elseif tbl12.gradient_position_type == "south" then
				n4 = 1.5707963267948966
			else
				n4 = 0

				if tbl12.gradient_position_type == "west" then
					n4 = 3.1415926535897931
				end
			end

			if fillFov then
				instance2.Visible = true
				instance2.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(vector2.X, vector2.Y)
				instance2.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(fovSize * 2, fovSize * 2)
				instance2.ImageTransparency = fillTransparency

				if not tbl20 or tbl20[1] ~= tbl12.fill_color_1 or tbl20[2] ~= tbl12.fill_color_2 or tbl20[3] ~= tbl12.fill_color_3 then
					tbl20 = { tbl12.fill_color_1, tbl12.fill_color_2, tbl12.fill_color_3 }
					local v6 = instance3
					local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new
					local tbl21 = {}
					local v7 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, tbl20[1])
					local v8 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, tbl20[2])
					local new = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
					local v9 = tbl20[3]
					tbl21[1] = v7
					tbl21[2] = v8

					do
						local values = table.pack(new(1, v9))
						table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, tbl21)
					end

					v6.Color = colorSequence(tbl21)
				end

				if tbl12.rotate_gradient and tbl12.rotation_targets["apply to fill circle"] then
					if tbl12.rotation_mode == "rotate to left" then
						instance3.Rotation = -(n3 * 25)
					elseif tbl12.rotation_mode == "rotate to right" then
						instance3.Rotation = n3 * 25
					elseif tbl12.rotation_mode == "forward and backward" then
						instance3.Rotation = math.sin(n3) * 180
					end
				end
			else
				instance2.Visible = false
			end

			for i = 1, 64 do
				local v6 = tbl19.main[i]
				local v7 = tbl19.inner[i]
				local v8 = tbl19.outer[i]
				local n5 = n4 + (i - 1) * 6.2831853071795862 / n2
				local n6 = n4 + i * 6.2831853071795862 / n2
				local n7 = i / n2
				local v9

				if tbl12.rotate_gradient and tbl12.rotation_targets["apply to outline circle"] then
					v9 = fn57(n7, n3)
				else
					v9 = n7
				end

				local sin = math.sin
				local from = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n5), sin(n5)) * fovSize
				local sin2 = math.sin
				local to = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n6), sin2(n6)) * fovSize

				if showFov then
					local sin3 = math.sin
					local from2 = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n5), sin3(n5)) * (fovSize - 1)
					local sin4 = math.sin
					local to2 = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n6), sin4(n6)) * (fovSize - 1)
					local sin5 = math.sin
					local from3 = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n5), sin5(n5)) * (fovSize + 1)
					local sin6 = math.sin
					local to3 = vector2 + Vector2.new(math.cos(n6), sin6(n6)) * (fovSize + 1)
					v6.From = from
					v6.To = to
					v6.Visible = true
					v6.Color = fn54(tbl12.outline_color_1, tbl12.outline_color_2, tbl12.outline_color_3, v9)
					v7.From = from2
					v7.To = to2
					v7.Visible = true
					v8.From = from3
					v8.To = to3
					v8.Visible = true
				else
					v6.Visible = false
					v7.Visible = false
					v8.Visible = false
				end
			end
		end

		local silentAimAutoshoot = tbl12.silent_aim and tbl12.silent_aim_autoshoot
		local flag6 = false

		if silentAimAutoshoot then
			local character = localPlayer3.Character
			local flag7 = false

			if character then
				for _, v6 in character:GetChildren() do
					local isA = v6:IsA("Tool")

					if isA then
						isA = v6:GetAttribute("ToolType") == "Gun"
					end

					if isA then
						flag7 = true
						break
					end
				end
			end

			if flag7 then
				local v6 = fn56()

				if v6 and tbl12.friend_check then
					local ok, result = pcall(function()
						return localPlayer3:IsFriendsWith(v6.UserId)
					end)

					if ok and result then
						v6 = nil
					end
				end

				if v6 and v6.Character and v6.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v6.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
					flag6 = true
				end
			end
		end

		if flag6 then
			if not flag5 then
				flag5 = true

				task.spawn(function()
					while true do
						if flag5 and tbl12.silent_aim and tbl12.silent_aim_autoshoot then
							local v6 = fn56()

							if not v6 or not v6.Character or not v6.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or v6.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
								flag5 = false
								break
							else
								sendMouseButtonEvent(VirtualInputManager, mouse_pos.X, mouse_pos.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
								task.wait(0.01)
								sendMouseButtonEvent(VirtualInputManager, mouse_pos.X, mouse_pos.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
								task.wait(0.03)
								continue
							end
						end

						break
					end

					flag5 = false
				end)
			end
		else
			flag5 = false
		end
	end)

	local instance4 = Instance.new("Sound", getgenv().sound_service or game:GetService("SoundService"))

	local function fn58()
		if not Toggles.hitsound.Value then
			return
		end
		local v7 = tbl23[Options.sound_type.Value]
		if not v7 then
			return
		end

		if v7:find("http") then

			if not isfolder("SillyScripts/Prison_Life") then
				makefolder("SillyScripts")
				makefolder("SillyScripts/Prison_Life")
			end

			local str = "SillyScripts/Prison_Life" .. "/" .. Options.sound_type.Value .. (v7:find(".ogg") and ".ogg" or ".mp3")

			if not isfile(str) then
				pcall(function()
					writefile(str, game:HttpGet(v7))
				end)
			end

			if isfile(str) then
				instance4.SoundId = getcustomasset(str)
			end
		else
			instance4.SoundId = v7
		end

		instance4.Volume = Options.sound_volume.Value / 10
		instance4:Play()
	end

	local function fn59(arg)
		if not Toggles.hiteffect.Value then
			return
		end

		if not arg then
			return
		end
		local v7 = tbl21[string.lower(Options.effect_type.Value or "")]

		if v7 then
			v7(arg)
		end
	end

	local function fn60(arg)
		if not arg then
			return false
		end

		while arg and arg ~= Workspace do
			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player.Character == arg then
					return true
				end
			end

			arg = arg.Parent
		end

		return false
	end

	local raycastParams3 = RaycastParams.new()
	raycastParams3.CollisionGroup = "ClientBullet"
	raycastParams3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local v7 = nil

	v7 = hookfunction(tbl18.Bullet, function(...)
		local v8 = table.pack(...)
		local v9 = ...
		if not tbl12.silent_aim then
			return v7(table.unpack(v8, 1, v8.n))
		end
		local hitChance = tbl12.hit_chance
		if math.random(1, 100) > hitChance then
			return v7(table.unpack(v8, 1, v8.n))
		end
		local v10 = fn56()
		if not v10 or not v10.Character then
			return v7(table.unpack(v8, 1, v8.n))
		end
		local v11 = v10.Character:FindFirstChild(tbl12.target_part)
		if not v11 then
			return v7(table.unpack(v8, 1, v8.n))
		end
		local v12 = table.pack(table.unpack(v8, 1, v8.n))
		v12[2] = v11.Position

		if tbl12.wallbang then
			local character = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if character then
				local filterDescendantsInstances = { localPlayer3.Character }

				for _, v13 in Players:GetPlayers() do
					if v13.Character then
						table.insert(filterDescendantsInstances, v13.Character)
					end
				end

				raycastParams3.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterDescendantsInstances
				local hit = Workspace:Raycast(v12[2], v9 - v12[2], raycastParams3)

				if hit then
					local v13, v14 = tbl13:Scan(character.Position, v12[2], hit.Position + hit.Normal * 0.01, v11)

					if v13 then
						for k, v15 in debug.getstack(3) do
							if v15 == v9 then
								debug.setstack(3, k, v13)
							end
						end

						v12[1] = v13
						if v14 then
							return v11, v14
						end
					end
				end

				local raycastParams4 = RaycastParams.new()
				raycastParams4.CollisionGroup = "ClientBullet"
				raycastParams4.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				raycastParams4.FilterDescendantsInstances = filterDescendantsInstances
				local n3 = v12[2] - character.Position
				local unit = n3.Unit

				for i = 2, n3.Magnitude, 2 do
					local hit2 = Workspace:Raycast(character.Position + unit * i, Vector3.new(0, -50, 0), raycastParams4)

					if hit2 then
						local n4 = hit2.Position + hit2.Normal * 0.5

						if not Workspace:Raycast(n4, v12[2] - n4, raycastParams4) then
							local v13, v14 = tbl13:Scan(n4, v12[2], nil, v11)

							if v13 then
								for k, v15 in debug.getstack(3) do
									if v15 == v9 then
										debug.setstack(3, k, v13)
									end
								end

								v12[1] = v13
								if v14 then
									return v11, v14
								end
								return v7(unpack(v12, 1, v12.n))
							end
						end
					end
				end

				for i = 3, 18, 3 do
					local n4 = character.Position + Vector3.new(0, i, 0)

					if not Workspace:Raycast(n4, v12[2] - n4, raycastParams4) then
						local v13, v14 = tbl13:Scan(n4, v12[2], nil, v11)

						if v13 then
							for k, v15 in debug.getstack(3) do
								if v15 == v9 then
									debug.setstack(3, k, v13)
								end
							end

							v12[1] = v13
							if v14 then
								return v11, v14
							end
							return v7(unpack(v12, 1, v12.n))
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return v7(unpack(v12, 1, v12.n))
	end)

	local v8 = nil

	if filtergc then
		v8 = nil

		for _, v9 in ipairs(filtergc("function", { Name = "castRay" })) do
			if not isexecutorclosure(v9) then
				local v10 = debug.getinfo(v9)

				if v10.source and v10.source:find("GunController") then
					v8 = v9
					break
				else
					v8 = nil
				end
			else
				v8 = nil
			end
		end
	end

	if not v8 then
		for _, v9 in pairs(getgc()) do

			if type(v9) == "function" and not isexecutorclosure(v9) then
				local v10 = debug.getinfo(v9)
				if v10.name == "castRay" and v10.source and v10.source:find("GunController") then
					v8 = v9
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if v8 then
		local v9 = nil

		local function fn61(...)
			local value = Options.effect_trigger and Options.effect_trigger.Value or "hitting"
			local value2 = Options.sound_trigger and Options.sound_trigger.Value or "hitting"

			if tbl12.silent_aim then
				local hitChance = tbl12.hit_chance

				if math.random(1, 100) <= hitChance then
					local v10 = fn56()

					if v10 and v10.Character then
						local v11 = v10.Character:FindFirstChild(tbl12.target_part)

						if v11 then
							if value == "shooting" or value == "hitting" then
								fn59(v11.Position)
							end

							if value2 == "shooting" or value2 == "hitting" then
								task.spawn(fn58)
							end

							return v11, v11.Position
						end
					end
				end
			end

			local tbl24 = { v9(...) }
			local v10 = tbl24[1]
			local v11 = tbl24[2]

			if value == "shooting" then
				if v11 then
					fn59(v11)
				end
			elseif value == "hitting" then
				if v10 and v11 and fn60(v10) then
					fn59(v11)
				end
			end

			if value2 == "shooting" then
				if v11 then
					task.spawn(fn58)
				end
			elseif value2 == "hitting" then
				if v10 and v11 and fn60(v10) then
					task.spawn(fn58)
				end
			end

			return unpack(tbl24)
		end

		v9 = hookfunction
		v9 = v9(v8, newcclosure(fn61))
	end
end

do
	local ragebot = tbl11.Main:AddLeftGroupbox("ragebot")
	local tbl15 = { tar = nil, act = false, wpn = "AK-47", org = nil, loop = nil, thrd = nil, cd = 0 }

	local function fn50()
		local tbl16 = {}

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			if player ~= localPlayer3 then
				table.insert(tbl16, player.Name)
			end
		end

		return tbl16
	end

	ragebot:AddDropdown("rage_target", {
		Text = "target",
		Default = 0,
		Values = fn50(),
		Callback = function(tar)
			tbl15.tar = tar
		end,
	})

	ragebot:AddDropdown("rage_guntouse", {
		Text = "gun to use",
		Default = 1,
		Values = { "AK-47", "MP5", "M9", "Remington 870" },
		Callback = function(wpn)
			tbl15.wpn = wpn
		end,
	})

	local function fn51(cFrame)
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if character then
			character.Velocity = Vector3.zero
			character.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
			character.CFrame = cFrame
		end
	end

	local function fn52()
		if not tbl15.act or not tbl15.tar then
			return nil
		end
		local v3 = Players:FindFirstChild(tbl15.tar)

		if v3 and v3.Character then
			local humanoid = v3.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
			local humanoidRootPart = v3.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local forceField = v3.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField")
			if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoidRootPart and not forceField then
				return v3.Character
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn53()
		local viewportSize = currentCamera.ViewportSize
		local n = viewportSize.X / 2
		local n2 = viewportSize.Y / 2

		while tbl15.act and fn52() do
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(n, n2, 0, true, game, 1)
			task.wait()
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(n, n2, 0, false, game, 1)
			task.wait()
		end
	end

	local value = nil
	local value2 = nil

	local function fn54()
		if not tbl15.act then
			return
		end
		tbl15.act = false

		if tbl15.loop then
			tbl15.loop:Disconnect()
			tbl15.loop = nil
		end

		if value ~= nil and Toggles.click_autofire then
			Toggles.click_autofire:SetValue(value)
		end

		if value2 ~= nil and Toggles.no_firerate2 then
			Toggles.no_firerate2:SetValue(value2)
		end
	end

	ragebot:AddButton("ragebot", function()
		local v3 = v2()

		if v3 < tbl15.cd then
			local v4 = math.ceil(tbl15.cd - v3)
			Library:Notify(string.format("[ragebot] - you need to wait %d more seconds to ragebot again", v4))
			return
		end

		if tbl15.act then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		local backpack = localPlayer3:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
		if not (character and character:FindFirstChild(tbl15.wpn) or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(tbl15.wpn)) then
			Library:Notify("you need to have " .. tbl15.wpn .. " in your inventory", 4)
			return
		end
		value = Toggles.click_autofire and Toggles.click_autofire.Value
		value2 = Toggles.no_firerate2 and Toggles.no_firerate2.Value

		if Toggles.click_autofire and not value then
			Toggles.click_autofire:SetValue(true)
		end

		if Toggles.no_firerate2 and not value2 then
			Toggles.no_firerate2:SetValue(true)
		end

		character = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if character then
			tbl15.org = character.CFrame
		end

		tbl15.act = true
		local flag3 = false

		if tbl15.loop then
			tbl15.loop:Disconnect()
		end

		tbl15.loop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not tbl15.act then
				return
			end
			local character2 = localPlayer3.Character
			character2 = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if not character2 or character2.Health <= 0 then
				fn54()
				tbl15.org = nil
				tbl15.cd = v2() + 30
				return
			end

			local v4 = fn52()

			if v4 then
				flag3 = true
				local humanoidRootPart = v4:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart then
					fn51(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3.5))
					local character3 = localPlayer3.Character
					local backpack2 = localPlayer3:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

					if character3 and backpack2 then
						local v5 = backpack2:FindFirstChild(tbl15.wpn)

						if v5 then
							v5.Parent = character3
						end
					end

					local flag4 = not tbl15.thrd

					if not flag4 then
						flag4 = coroutine.status(tbl15.thrd) == "dead"
					end

					if flag4 then
						tbl15.thrd = coroutine.create(fn53)
						coroutine.resume(tbl15.thrd)
					end
				end
			elseif flag3 then
				fn54()

				task.delay(1.5, function()
					if tbl15.org then
						fn51(tbl15.org)
					end

					pcall(fn49)
					tbl15.org = nil
					tbl15.cd = v2() + 30
				end)
			end
		end)
	end)

	ragebot:AddButton("stop ragebot", function()
		fn54()

		if tbl15.org then
			fn51(tbl15.org)
			pcall(fn49)
			tbl15.org = nil
			tbl15.cd = v2() + 30
		end
	end)

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
		Options.rage_target:SetValues(fn50())
	end)

			fn54()

					fn51(tbl15.org)
				end

				pcall(fn49)
				tbl15.org = nil
				tbl15.cd = v2() + 30
			end)
		end

		Options.rage_target:SetValues(fn50())
	end)

	for _, v4 in filtergc("function", { Name = "castRay" }) do
		if not isexecutorclosure(v4) then
			local v5 = debug.getinfo(v4)

			if v5.source and v5.source:find("GunController") then
				v3 = v4
				break
			else
				v3 = nil
			end
		else
			v3 = nil
		end
	end

	if not v3 then
		for _, v4 in getgc() do

			if type(v4) == "function" and not isexecutorclosure(v4) then
				local v5 = debug.getinfo(v4)
				if v5.name == "castRay" and v5.source and v5.source:find("GunController") then
					v3 = v4
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if v3 then
		local v4 = nil

		local function fn55(...)
			if tbl15.act then
				local v5 = fn52()

				if v5 then
					local head = v5:FindFirstChild("Head") or v5:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if head then
						return head, head.Position
					end
				end
			end

			return v4(...)
		end

		v4 = hookfunction
		v4 = v4(v3, newcclosure(fn55))
	end
end

do
	local aimbot = tbl11.Main:AddRightGroupbox("aimbot")

	getgenv().camlock_settings = getgenv().camlock_settings or {
		enabled = false,
		target = nil,
		smooth_toggle = false,
		smooth_val = 0.5,
		part = "Head",
		pred_toggle = false,
		px = 0,
		py = 0,
		pz = 0,
		shake_toggle = false,
		shake_val = 0,
		friend_check = false,
		blacklisted_teams = {
			["Criminals"] = false,
			["Guards"] = false,
			["Inmates"] = false,
			["Neutral"] = false,
		},
	}

	local function fn50()
		local camlockSettings = getgenv().camlock_settings
		if not camlockSettings.enabled then
			return
		end
		local mouseLocation = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
		local players = Players:GetPlayers()
		local huge = math.huge
		local v3 = nil

		for i = 1, #players do
			local v4 = players[i]

			if v4 ~= localPlayer3 and v4.Character then
				local team = v4.Team

				if not camlockSettings.blacklisted_teams[team and team.Name or "Neutral"] then
					if camlockSettings.friend_check then
						local ok, result = pcall(function()
							return localPlayer3:IsFriendsWith(v4.UserId)
						end)

						if ok and result then
							continue
						end
					end

					local v5 = v4.Character:FindFirstChild(camlockSettings.part)

					if v5 then
						local v6, v7 = currentCamera:WorldToViewportPoint(v5.Position)

						if v7 then
							local magnitude = (Vector2.new(mouseLocation.X, mouseLocation.Y) - Vector2.new(v6.X, v6.Y)).Magnitude

							if magnitude < huge then
								v3 = v4
								huge = magnitude
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		camlockSettings.target = v3
	end

RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
	local camlockSettings = getgenv().camlock_settings
	if not camlockSettings.enabled or not camlockSettings.target then
		return
	end
	local character = camlockSettings.target.Character
	if not character or not localPlayer3.Character then
		return
	end
	local team = camlockSettings.target.Team
	if camlockSettings.blacklisted_teams[team and team.Name or "Neutral"] then
		camlockSettings.target = nil
		return
	end

	if camlockSettings.friend_check then
		local ok, result = pcall(function()
			return localPlayer3:IsFriendsWith(camlockSettings.target.UserId)
		end)

		if ok and result then
			camlockSettings.target = nil
			return
		end
	end

	local v3 = character:FindFirstChild(camlockSettings.part)
	if not v3 then
		return
	end
	local position = v3.Position

	if camlockSettings.pred_toggle then
		local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if humanoidRootPart then
			local velocity = humanoidRootPart.Velocity
			position += Vector3.new(velocity.X * camlockSettings.px, velocity.Y * camlockSettings.py, velocity.Z * camlockSettings.pz)
		end
	end

	local unit = (position - currentCamera.CFrame.Position).Unit

	if camlockSettings.shake_toggle and camlockSettings.shake_val > 0 then
		local n = camlockSettings.shake_val * 0.02
		unit = (unit + Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * n, (math.random() - 0.5) * n, (math.random() - 0.5) * n)).Unit
	end

	if camlockSettings.smooth_toggle then
		local cFrame = currentCamera.CFrame
		currentCamera.CFrame = cFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(cFrame.Position, cFrame.Position + unit), 1 - camlockSettings.smooth_val)
	else
		currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(currentCamera.CFrame.Position, currentCamera.CFrame.Position + unit)
	end
end)

	local function fn50()
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		character = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		character = character and character.SeatPart
		if character and character:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
			return character
		end
		local carContainer = Workspace:FindFirstChild("CarContainer")
		if not carContainer then
			return nil
		end

		for _, v4 in carContainer:GetDescendants() do
			local isA = v4:IsA("VehicleSeat")

			if isA then
				isA = typeof(isnetworkowner) == "function"
			end

			if isA and isnetworkowner(v4) then
				return v4
			end
		end

		return nil
	end

	local function fn52(arg)
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
		end

		local kickExploitState = getgenv().kick_exploit_state

		if not arg then
			if kickExploitState.humanoid and kickExploitState.humanoid.Parent and kickExploitState.jump_enabled ~= nil then
				kickExploitState.humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, kickExploitState.jump_enabled)
			end

			kickExploitState.saved_cf = nil
			kickExploitState.target_seen = false
			kickExploitState.target_sent = false
			kickExploitState.jump_enabled = nil
			kickExploitState.humanoid = nil
			return
		end

		kickExploitState.saved_cf = nil
		kickExploitState.target_seen = false
		kickExploitState.target_sent = false
		local character = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

		if character then
			kickExploitState.humanoid = character
			kickExploitState.jump_enabled = character:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
			character:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
		end

		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not Toggles.kick_exploit_enabled or not Toggles.kick_exploit_enabled.Value then
				return
			end
			local kickExploitState2 = getgenv().kick_exploit_state
			local character2 = localPlayer3.Character
			local humanoid = character2 and character2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid then
				if kickExploitState2.humanoid ~= humanoid then
					kickExploitState2.humanoid = humanoid
					kickExploitState2.jump_enabled = humanoid:GetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
				end

				humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
				humanoid.Jump = false
			end

			local v4 = fn50()
			if not v4 then
				return
			end

			if not kickExploitState2.saved_cf then
				kickExploitState2.saved_cf = v4.CFrame
			end

			local v5 = fn51()
			local character3 = v5 and v5.Character and v5.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

			if character3 then
				kickExploitState2.target_seen = true

				if character3.Position.Y < -100 then
					kickExploitState2.target_sent = true
				end
			elseif kickExploitState2.target_seen then
				kickExploitState2.target_sent = true
			end

			if kickExploitState2.target_sent and kickExploitState2.saved_cf and character2 then
				local humanoidRootPart = character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if humanoidRootPart and (humanoidRootPart.Position - kickExploitState2.saved_cf.Position).Magnitude > 150 then
					v4.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					v4.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
					v4.CFrame = kickExploitState2.saved_cf
					kickExploitState2.target_seen = false
					kickExploitState2.target_sent = false
				end
			end

			if not character3 then
				return
			end
			v4.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(10000, 10000, 0)
			v4.CFrame = CFrame.new(character3.Position) * CFrame.new(-2, -2, -12)

			if typeof(sethiddenproperty) == "function" then

		end)

	local function fn59()
		while flag5 do
			local character = localPlayer3.Character
			local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

			if humanoid and humanoid.Health <= 85 and humanoid.Health > 0 then
				local backpack = localPlayer3:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Backpack")
				local v5 = nil

				if backpack then
					v5 = nil

					for _, v6 in backpack:GetChildren() do
						if tbl16[v6.Name] then
							v5 = v6
							break
						else
							v5 = nil
						end
					end
				end

				local flag6

				if v5 then
					flag6 = os.clock() - (v5:GetAttribute("Client_LastConsumedAt") or 0) >= 3
				else
					flag6 = v5
				end

				if flag6 then
					local tool = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")

					if tool then
						tool.Parent = backpack
					end

					v5.Parent = character
					v5:SetAttribute("Quantity", (v5:GetAttribute("Quantity") or 1) - 1)
					v5:SetAttribute("Client_LastConsumedAt", os.clock())
					library:Notify("auto heal", "quantity: " .. tostring(v5:GetAttribute("Quantity")), 3)
					ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.EatFood:FireServer()

					if v5 and v5.Parent then
						v5.Parent = backpack
					end

					if tool and localPlayer3.Character and tool.Parent then
						tool.Parent = localPlayer3.Character
					end
				end
			end

			task.wait(0.05)
		end
	end

		end)
	end

	local function fn63(arg, arg2)
		if not tbl17[arg.Animation.AnimationId] and arg2 then
			if tbl18[arg] then
				task.cancel(tbl18[arg])
			end

			local v6 = v2()

			if not tbl20[arg2.UserId] or v6 - tbl20[arg2.UserId] >= 5 then
				tbl20[arg2.UserId] = v6
				Library:Notify("resolve invisible", "resolved invisible for " .. arg2.Name, 3)
			end

			tbl18[arg] = task.spawn(function()
				while true do
					pcall(function()
						arg:AdjustWeight(0, 0)
					end)

					task.wait()
					if arg.IsPlaying and flag7 then
						continue
					end
					break
				end

				tbl18[arg] = nil
			end)
		end
	end

	local function fn64(arg, arg2)
		if arg2 == localPlayer3 then
			return
		end
		local animator = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
		animator = animator and animator:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)

		if animator and flag7 then
			table.insert(tbl19, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(arg3)
				fn63(arg3, arg2)
			end))

			for _, v6 in animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do
				task.spawn(fn63, v6, arg2)
			end
		end
	end

					table.insert(tbl19, player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)

	misc:AddToggle("fling_cars", {
		Text = "fling cars",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			if arg then
				if connection5 then
					connection5:Disconnect()
					connection5 = nil
				end

				local service7 = game:GetService("RunService")
				local service8 = game:GetService("Players")
				local localPlayer4 = service8.LocalPlayer
				local squad = Workspace:WaitForChild("CarContainer"):WaitForChild("Squad")

				if not squad.PrimaryPart then
					squad.PrimaryPart = squad:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
				end

				for _, descendant in ipairs(squad:GetDescendants()) do
					if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
						descendant.CanCollide = false
					end
				end

				local function fn65()
					local character = localPlayer4.Character and localPlayer4.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not character then
						return nil
					end
					local huge = math.huge
					local v6 = nil

					for _, player in ipairs(service8:GetPlayers()) do
						if player ~= localPlayer4 and player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local magnitude = (player.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - character.Position).Magnitude

							if magnitude < huge then
								huge = magnitude
								v6 = player
							end
						end
					end

					return v6
				end

				connection5 = service7.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if not Toggles.fling_cars.Value or not squad.PrimaryPart then
						return
					end
					local v6 = fn65()

					if v6 and v6.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
						local random = math.random
						local n2 = v6.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Vector3.new(0, -3, 0) + Vector3.new(math.random(-2, 2), 0, random(-2, 2))
						local rad = math.rad
						local random2 = math.random
						local cframe = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(math.random(-45, 45)), 0, rad(random2(-45, 45)))
						squad:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(squad.PrimaryPart.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(n2, n2 + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)) * cframe, 0.5))
						local random3 = math.random
						squad.PrimaryPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(math.random(-1, 1), 1, random3(-1, 1)) * 800
					end
				end)
			elseif connection5 then
				connection5:Disconnect()
				connection5 = nil
			end
		end,
	})

	local function fn51(arg)
		local v4 = tbl15[arg]
		if not v4 then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		local humanoidRootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not humanoidRootPart then
			return
		end
		local cFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
		local v5 = v2()

		task.spawn(function()
			repeat
				local vector = Vector3.new(math.random(-5, 5) / 10, math.random(-5, 5) / 10, math.random(-5, 5) / 10)
				humanoidRootPart.CFrame = v4.cframe * CFrame.new(vector)
				task.wait()
			until v2() - v5 > 0.8

			humanoidRootPart.CFrame = cFrame
		end)

		task.wait(0.5)

		pcall(function()
			ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("InteractWithItem"):InvokeServer(unpack(v4.args))
		end)
	end

				end

				hiddenAssets.Towers = CollectionService:GetTagged("GuardTowers")

				for _, tower in ipairs(hiddenAssets.Towers) do

				end
			else

				end

		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			local cellDoors = Workspace:FindFirstChild("CellDoors")

			if arg then
				if cellDoors then
					hiddenAssets.Doors = cellDoors
					cellDoors.Parent = nil
				end
			elseif hiddenAssets.Doors then
				hiddenAssets.Doors.Parent = Workspace
			end
		end,
	})

	v4:AddToggle("CityBuildings", {
		Text = "remove city buildings",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			local cityBuildings = Workspace:FindFirstChild("City_buildings")

			if arg then
				if cityBuildings then
					hiddenAssets.Buildings = cityBuildings
					cityBuildings.Parent = nil
				end
			elseif hiddenAssets.Buildings then
				hiddenAssets.Buildings.Parent = Workspace
			end
		end,
	})

	v4:AddToggle("Warehouses", {
		Text = "remove criminal warehouse",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			local warehouse1 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Warehouse1")
			local warehouse2 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Warehouse2")

			if arg then
				if warehouse1 then
					hiddenAssets.W1 = warehouse1
					warehouse1.Parent = nil
				end

				if warehouse2 then
					hiddenAssets.W2 = warehouse2
					warehouse2.Parent = nil
				end
			else
				if hiddenAssets.W1 then
					hiddenAssets.W1.Parent = Workspace
				end

				if hiddenAssets.W2 then
					hiddenAssets.W2.Parent = Workspace
				end
			end
		end,
	})
end

do
	local v4 = nil

	local function fn50(arg, ...)
		local tbl15 = { ... }

		if getnamecallmethod() == "GetAttributes" and not checkcaller() then
			local v5 = v4(arg, unpack(tbl15))

			if typeof(v5) == "table" then
				if Toggles.click_autofire and Toggles.click_autofire.Value then
					v5.AutoFire = true
				end

				if Toggles.no_firerate and Toggles.no_firerate.Value then
					v5.FireRate = 0
				elseif Toggles.no_firerate2 and Toggles.no_firerate2.Value then
					v5.FireRate = 0.05
				elseif Toggles.no_firerate3 and Toggles.no_firerate3.Value then
					v5.FireRate = 0.1
				end

				if Toggles.no_spread and Toggles.no_spread.Value then
					v5.Spread = 0
				end

				if Options.firerange then
					v5.Range = Options.firerange.Value
				end

				return v5
			end
		end

		return v4(arg, unpack(tbl15))
	end

	v4 = hookmetamethod
	v4 = v4(game, "__namecall", fn50)
end

local v4 = tbl11.Misc:AddRightGroupbox("gun modifications")

v4:AddToggle("click_autofire", {
	Text = "hold click to autofire",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn49()
		else
			fn49()
		end
	end,
})

v4:AddToggle("no_firerate", {
	Text = "no fire rate (instant)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn49()
			Toggles.no_firerate2:SetValue(false)
			Toggles.no_firerate3:SetValue(false)
		else
			fn49()
		end
	end,
})

v4:AddToggle("no_firerate2", {
	Text = "no fire rate (medium)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn49()
			Toggles.no_firerate:SetValue(false)
			Toggles.no_firerate3:SetValue(false)
		else
			fn49()
		end
	end,
})

v4:AddToggle("no_firerate3", {
	Text = "no fire rate (slightly)",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn49()
			Toggles.no_firerate:SetValue(false)
			Toggles.no_firerate2:SetValue(false)
		else
			fn49()
		end
	end,
})

v4:AddToggle("no_spread", {
	Text = "no fire spread",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(arg)
		if arg then
			fn49()
		else
			fn49()
		end
	end,
})

v4:AddSlider("firerange", {
	Text = "fire range",
	Default = 1000,
	Min = 0,
	Max = 100000,
	Rounding = 0,
	Compact = true,
	Callback = function()
		fn49()
	end,
})

do
	local movement = tbl11.Misc:AddRightGroupbox("movement")

	local tbl15 = {
		walkspeed = false,
		walkspeed_multiplier = 1,
		jumppower = false,
		jumppower_multiplier = 1,
		spin_bot = false,
		spin_speed = 50,
		char_fly = false,
		char_fly_speed = 15,
		vehicle_fly = false,
		vehicle_fly_speed = 400,
		max_height = 49.9,
		turn_speed = 0.15,
	}

	local tbl16 = { current_vehicle = nil, bg = nil, bv = nil }
	local tbl17 = { bg = nil, bv = nil }
	local raycastParams2 = RaycastParams.new()
	raycastParams2.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local raycastParams3 = RaycastParams.new()
	raycastParams3.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	local connection = nil
	local n = 15
	local n2 = 50
	local n3 = 0

	local function fn50()
		if tbl16.bg then
			tbl16.bg:Destroy()
			tbl16.bg = nil
		end

		if tbl16.bv then
			tbl16.bv:Destroy()
			tbl16.bv = nil
		end

		tbl16.current_vehicle = nil
	end

	local function fn51()
		if tbl17.bg then
			tbl17.bg:Destroy()
			tbl17.bg = nil
		end

		if tbl17.bv then
			tbl17.bv:Destroy()
			tbl17.bv = nil
		end

		tbl15.char_fly = false
	end

	local function fn52(arg)
		local humanoid = arg:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		local humanoidRootPart = arg:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
		end

		raycastParams2.FilterDescendantsInstances = { arg }

		connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
			if not (humanoid and humanoid.Parent and humanoidRootPart and humanoidRootPart.Parent) then
				connection:Disconnect()
				return
			end

			if tbl15.walkspeed then
				local walkSpeed = n * tbl15.walkspeed_multiplier

				if humanoid.WalkSpeed ~= walkSpeed then
					humanoid.WalkSpeed = walkSpeed
				end
			end

			if tbl15.jumppower then
				local jumpPower = n2 * tbl15.jumppower_multiplier

				if not humanoid.UseJumpPower then
					humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
				end

				if humanoid.JumpPower ~= jumpPower then
					humanoid.JumpPower = jumpPower
				end
			end

			if tbl15.spin_bot then
				local parent = humanoidRootPart.Parent and humanoidRootPart.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if parent then
					parent.AutoRotate = false
				end

				if not spin_joint or not spin_joint.Parent then
					spin_joint = humanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("RootJoint")
					spin_original_c0 = spin_joint and spin_joint.C0
				end

				n3 = (n3 + tbl15.spin_speed * 16 * deltaTime) % 360

				if spin_joint and spin_original_c0 then
					local v5 = spin_original_c0
					spin_joint.C0 = CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(n3), 0) * v5
				end
			else
				local parent = humanoidRootPart.Parent and humanoidRootPart.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if parent then
					parent.AutoRotate = true
				end

				if spin_joint and spin_original_c0 and spin_joint.Parent then
					spin_joint.C0 = spin_original_c0
				end

				n3 = 0
				spin_joint = nil
				spin_original_c0 = nil
			end

			if tbl15.char_fly then
				if not tbl17.bv then
					tbl17.bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
					tbl17.bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
					tbl17.bv.Parent = humanoidRootPart
					tbl17.bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
					tbl17.bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e9, 1e9, 1e9)
					tbl17.bg.P = 5000
					tbl17.bg.D = 500
					tbl17.bg.CFrame = humanoidRootPart.CFrame
					tbl17.bg.Parent = humanoidRootPart
				end

				local unit = Vector3.new(currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
				local unit2 = Vector3.new(currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
				local vector = Vector3.zero

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
					vector = Vector3.zero + unit
				end

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
					vector -= unit
				end

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
					vector -= unit2
				end

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
					vector += unit2
				end

				local n4

				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
					n4 = 1
				else
					n4 = 0

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then
						n4 = -1
					end
				end

				local vector2 = vector.Magnitude > 0 and vector.Unit * tbl15.char_fly_speed or Vector3.zero
				local n5 = n4 * tbl15.char_fly_speed
				local hit = Workspace:Raycast(humanoidRootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), raycastParams2)
				hit = hit and humanoidRootPart.Position.Y - hit.Position.Y or 0

				if hit >= tbl15.max_height and n5 > 0 then
					n5 = 0
				end

				if tbl15.max_height + 2 < hit then
					n5 = -10
				end

				tbl17.bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(vector2.X, n5, vector2.Z)

				if vector.Magnitude > 0 then
					local turnSpeed = tbl15.turn_speed
					tbl17.bg.CFrame = tbl17.bg.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, vector.Unit, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), turnSpeed)
				end
			elseif tbl17.bv then
				tbl17.bv:Destroy()
				tbl17.bv = nil
				tbl17.bg:Destroy()
				tbl17.bg = nil
			end

			if tbl15.vehicle_fly then
				local seatPart = humanoid.SeatPart

				if seatPart and seatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
					if not tbl16.bv then
						tbl16.bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
						tbl16.bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1000000, 1000000, 1000000)
						tbl16.bv.Parent = seatPart
						tbl16.bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
						tbl16.bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1000000, 1000000, 1000000)
						tbl16.bg.P = 5000
						tbl16.bg.D = 500
						tbl16.bg.CFrame = seatPart.CFrame
						tbl16.bg.Parent = seatPart
						tbl16.current_vehicle = seatPart.Parent
						raycastParams3.FilterDescendantsInstances = { seatPart.Parent, arg }
					end

					if tbl16.current_vehicle ~= seatPart.Parent then
						tbl16.current_vehicle = seatPart.Parent
						raycastParams3.FilterDescendantsInstances = { seatPart.Parent, arg }
					end

					local hit = Workspace:Raycast(seatPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -500, 0), raycastParams3)
					hit = hit and seatPart.Position.Y - hit.Position.Y or 0
					local unit = Vector3.new(currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, currentCamera.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
					local unit2 = Vector3.new(currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.X, 0, currentCamera.CFrame.RightVector.Z).Unit
					local vector = Vector3.zero

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then
						vector = Vector3.zero + unit
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then
						vector -= unit
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then
						vector -= unit2
					end

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then
						vector += unit2
					end

					local n4

					if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.E) then
						n4 = 1
					else
						n4 = 0

						if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Q) then
							n4 = -1
						end
					end

					local vector2 = vector.Magnitude > 0 and vector.Unit * tbl15.vehicle_fly_speed or Vector3.zero
					local n5 = n4 * tbl15.vehicle_fly_speed

					if hit >= tbl15.max_height and n5 > 0 then
						n5 = 0
					end

					if tbl15.max_height + 2 < hit then
						n5 = -10
					end

					tbl16.bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(vector2.X, n5, vector2.Z)

					if vector.Magnitude > 0 then
						local turnSpeed = tbl15.turn_speed
						tbl16.bg.CFrame = tbl16.bg.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.zero, vector.Unit, Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)), turnSpeed)
					end
				elseif tbl16.current_vehicle then
					fn50()
				end
			elseif tbl16.current_vehicle then
				fn50()
			end
		end)
	end

	movement:AddToggle("walkspeed", {
		Text = "walkspeed",
		Default = tbl15.walkspeed,
		Callback = function(walkspeed)
			tbl15.walkspeed = walkspeed

			if not walkspeed and localPlayer3.Character then
				local humanoid = localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid then
					humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	movement:AddSlider("walkspeed_multiplier", {
		Text = "multiplier",
		Default = 1,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 2,
		Suffix = "x",
		Rounding = 2,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(walkspeedMultiplier)
			tbl15.walkspeed_multiplier = walkspeedMultiplier
		end,
	})

	movement:AddToggle("jumpheight", {
		Text = "jump power",
		Default = tbl15.jumppower,
		Callback = function(jumppower)
			tbl15.jumppower = jumppower

			if not jumppower and localPlayer3.Character then
				local humanoid = localPlayer3.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if humanoid then
					humanoid.JumpPower = 50
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	movement:AddSlider("jumpheight_multiplier", {
		Text = "multiplier",
		Default = 1,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 2.3,
		Suffix = "x",
		Rounding = 2,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(jumppowerMultiplier)
			tbl15.jumppower_multiplier = jumppowerMultiplier
		end,
	})

	movement:AddToggle("spin_bot", {
		Text = "spin bot",
		Default = tbl15.spin_bot,
		Callback = function(spinBot)
			tbl15.spin_bot = spinBot
		end,
	})

	movement:AddSlider("spin_speed", {
		Text = "spin speed",
		Default = 50,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 100,
		Suffix = "",
		Rounding = 1,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(spinSpeed)
			tbl15.spin_speed = spinSpeed
		end,
	})

	movement:AddToggle("char_fly", {
		Text = "fly",
		Default = tbl15.char_fly,
		Callback = function(charFly)
			tbl15.char_fly = charFly

			if not charFly then
				fn51()
			end
		end,
	}):AddKeyPicker("char_fly_key", {
		Default = "",
		SyncToggleState = true,
		Mode = "Toggle",
		Text = "character fly",
		NoUI = false,
	})

	movement:AddSlider("char_fly_speed", {
		Text = "fly speed",
		Default = 10,
		Min = 10,
		Max = 30,
		Suffix = " studs",
		Rounding = 1,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(charFlySpeed)
			tbl15.char_fly_speed = charFlySpeed
		end,
	})

	movement:AddToggle("vehicle_fly", {
		Text = "vehicle fly",
		Default = tbl15.vehicle_fly,
		Callback = function(vehicleFly)
			tbl15.vehicle_fly = vehicleFly

			if not vehicleFly then
				fn50()
			end
		end,
	})

	movement:AddSlider("vehicle_fly_speed", {
		Text = "vehicle fly speed",
		Default = 400,
		Min = 1,
		Max = 800,
		Suffix = " studs",
		Rounding = 1,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(vehicleFlySpeed)
			tbl15.vehicle_fly_speed = vehicleFlySpeed
		end,
	})

	if localPlayer3.Character then
		fn52(localPlayer3.Character)
	end

	localPlayer3.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		fn52(character)

		if tbl15.char_fly then
			fn51()
		end

		if tbl15.vehicle_fly then
			fn50()
		end

		local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)

		if humanoid then
			humanoid.Died:Once(function()
				if Toggles.spin_bot and Toggles.spin_bot.Value then
					Toggles.spin_bot:SetValue(false)

					task.delay(0.06, function()
						if Toggles.spin_bot and Toggles.spin_bot.Value then
							Toggles.spin_bot:SetValue(true)
						end
					end)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

do
	local tbl15 = {
		esp_enabled = false,
		esp_box = false,
		box_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		box_fill_enabled = false,
		box_fill_color1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		box_fill_color2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		fill_transparency = 0.5,
		esp_healthbar = false,
		healthbar_position = "Left",
		healthbar_color1 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),
		healthbar_color2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),
		healthbar_color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
		healthbar_anim_speed = 0.3,
		healthbar_text_enabled = false,
		healthbar_text_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		esp_names = false,
		names_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		esp_distance = false,
		distance_color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
		esp_tool = false,
		tools_color = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
		tool_no_tool_text = "none",
		esp_flags = false,
		flags_offset = 4,
		team_check = false,
		team_use_colors = false,
	}

	local esp = tbl11.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox("esp")

	esp:AddToggle("esp_enabled", {
		Text = "enabled",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espEnabled)
			tbl15.esp_enabled = espEnabled
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_box", {
		Text = "boxes",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espBox)
			tbl15.esp_box = espBox
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("box_color", {
		Default = tbl15.box_color,
		Callback = function(boxColor)
			tbl15.box_color = boxColor
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("box_fill_enabled", {
		Text = "box fill",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(boxFillEnabled)
			tbl15.box_fill_enabled = boxFillEnabled
		end,
	})

	esp:AddLabel("box fill colors"):AddColorPicker("box_fill_color1", {
		Default = tbl15.box_fill_color1,
		Callback = function(boxFillColor1)
			tbl15.box_fill_color1 = boxFillColor1
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("box_fill_color2", {
		Default = tbl15.box_fill_color2,
		Callback = function(boxFillColor2)
			tbl15.box_fill_color2 = boxFillColor2
		end,
	})

	esp:AddSlider("fill_transparency", {
		Text = "fill transparency",
		Default = 0.5,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 1,
		Rounding = 2,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(fillTransparency)
			tbl15.fill_transparency = fillTransparency
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_healthbar", {
		Text = "health bars",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espHealthbar)
			tbl15.esp_healthbar = espHealthbar
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("healthbar_color1", {
		Default = tbl15.healthbar_color1,
		Callback = function(healthbarColor1)
			tbl15.healthbar_color1 = healthbarColor1
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("healthbar_color2", {
		Default = tbl15.healthbar_color2,
		Callback = function(healthbarColor2)
			tbl15.healthbar_color2 = healthbarColor2
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("healthbar_color3", {
		Default = tbl15.healthbar_color3,
		Callback = function(healthbarColor3)
			tbl15.healthbar_color3 = healthbarColor3
		end,
	})

	esp:AddDropdown("healthbar_position", {
		Text = "healthbar position",
		Default = "Left",
		Values = { "Left", "Right" },
		Callback = function(healthbarPosition)
			tbl15.healthbar_position = healthbarPosition
		end,
	})

	esp:AddSlider("healthbar_anim_speed", {
		Text = "healthbar animation",
		Default = 0.5,
		Min = 0.05,
		Max = 2,
		Rounding = 2,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(healthbarAnimSpeed)
			tbl15.healthbar_anim_speed = healthbarAnimSpeed
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("healthbar_text_enabled", {
		Text = "health text",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(healthbarTextEnabled)
			tbl15.healthbar_text_enabled = healthbarTextEnabled
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("healthbar_text_color", {
		Default = tbl15.healthbar_text_color,
		Callback = function(healthbarTextColor)
			tbl15.healthbar_text_color = healthbarTextColor
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_names", {
		Text = "names",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espNames)
			tbl15.esp_names = espNames
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("names_color", {
		Default = tbl15.names_color,
		Callback = function(namesColor)
			tbl15.names_color = namesColor
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_distance", {
		Text = "distance",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espDistance)
			tbl15.esp_distance = espDistance
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("distance_color", {
		Default = tbl15.distance_color,
		Callback = function(distanceColor)
			tbl15.distance_color = distanceColor
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_tool", {
		Text = "tools",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espTool)
			tbl15.esp_tool = espTool
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("tools_color", {
		Default = tbl15.tools_color,
		Callback = function(toolsColor)
			tbl15.tools_color = toolsColor
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("esp_flags", {
		Text = "flags",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(espFlags)
			tbl15.esp_flags = espFlags
		end,
	})

	esp:AddSlider("flags_offset", {
		Text = "flags offset",
		Default = 4,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 20,
		Rounding = 1,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(flagsOffset)
			tbl15.flags_offset = flagsOffset
		end,
	})

	esp:AddToggle("team_check", {
		Text = "team check",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(teamCheck)
			tbl15.team_check = teamCheck
		end,
	})

	fn53 = function(arg)
		local v5 = tbl16[arg]
		if not v5 then
			return
		end

		if v5.health_tween then
			v5.health_tween:Cancel()
			v5.health_tween = nil
		end

		if v5.holder then
			v5.holder:Destroy()
		end

		tbl16[arg] = nil
	end

	local function fn55(arg)
		local v5 = tbl17[arg]

		if v5 then
			for i = 1, #v5 do
				v5[i]:Disconnect()
			end

			tbl17[arg] = nil
		end
	end

	local function fn56(player)
		if player == localPlayer3 then
			return
		end
		fn55(player)
		local v5 = table.create(2)

		v5[1] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
			fn54(player, character)
		end)

		v5[2] = player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
			fn53(player)
		end)

		tbl17[player] = v5

		if player.Character then
			fn54(player, player.Character)
		end
	end

	for _, v5 in Players:GetPlayers() do
		fn56(v5)
	end

	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(fn56)

	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
		fn55(player)
		fn53(player)
	end)

	RunService.PreRender:Connect(function()
		local v5 = tbl15

		if not v5.esp_enabled then
			for _, v6 in tbl16, nil, nil do
				v6.holder.Visible = false
			end

			return
		end

		local currentCamera2 = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not currentCamera2 then
			return
		end
		local position = currentCamera2.CFrame.Position

		for k, v6 in tbl16, nil, nil do
			local character = k.Character

			if not character or character ~= v6.character or not character:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
				v6.holder.Visible = false
			else
				local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
				local humanoidRootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if not humanoid or not head or not humanoidRootPart or humanoid.Health <= 0 or humanoid.MaxHealth <= 0 then
					v6.holder.Visible = false
				elseif v5.team_check and localPlayer3.Team and k.Team == localPlayer3.Team then
					v6.holder.Visible = false
				else
					local v7, v8 = fn52(head, humanoidRootPart, currentCamera2)

					if not v7 or not v8 then
						v6.holder.Visible = false
					else
						v6.holder.Visible = true
						v6.holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.round(v7.X), math.round(v7.Y))
						v6.holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, math.round(v8.X)), math.max(1, math.round(v8.Y)))
						local team = v5.team_use_colors and k.Team
						local color = nil

						if team then
							color = k.TeamColor.Color
						end

						local boxColor = color or v5.box_color
						color = color or v5.box_fill_color1
						v6.box_outline.Visible = v5.esp_box
						v6.box_line.Visible = v5.esp_box
						v6.box_fill.Visible = v5.esp_box and v5.box_fill_enabled

						if v5.esp_box then
							v6.box_line_stroke.Color = boxColor

							if v5.box_fill_enabled then
								local new = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
								local boxFillColor2 = v5.box_fill_color2
								v6.box_gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color), new(1, boxFillColor2) })
								v6.box_gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(v5.fill_transparency)
							end
						end

						if v5.esp_healthbar then
							local lastHealth = math.clamp(humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
							v6.health_holder.AnchorPoint = v5.healthbar_position == "Left" and Vector2.new(1, 0) or Vector2.new(0, 0)
							v6.health_holder.Position = v5.healthbar_position == "Left" and UDim2.new(0, -3, 0, 0) or UDim2.new(1, 3, 0, 0)
							v6.health_holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0)
							v6.health_holder.Visible = true
							v6.health_outline.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
							local healthGradient = v6.health_gradient
							local colorSequence = ColorSequence.new
							local tbl19 = {}
							local v9 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, v5.healthbar_color1)
							local v10 = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, v5.healthbar_color2)
							local new = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
							local healthbarColor3 = v5.healthbar_color3
							tbl19[1] = v9
							tbl19[2] = v10

							do
								local values = table.pack(new(1, healthbarColor3))
								table.move(values, 1, values.n, 3, tbl19)
							end

							healthGradient.Color = colorSequence(tbl19)

							if math.abs(v6.last_health - lastHealth) > 0.0001 then
								if v6.health_tween then
									v6.health_tween:Cancel()
								end

								v6.health_tween = TweenService:Create(v6.health_fill, TweenInfo.new(v5.healthbar_anim_speed, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, lastHealth, 0) })
								v6.health_tween:Play()
								v6.last_health = lastHealth
							end

							if v5.healthbar_text_enabled then
								v6.health_text.Visible = true
								v6.health_text.Text = tostring(math.floor(humanoid.Health))
								v6.health_text.TextColor3 = v5.healthbar_text_color

								if v5.healthbar_position == "Left" then
									v6.health_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
									v6.health_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
									v6.health_text.Position = UDim2.new(0, -3, 1 - lastHealth, 0)
								else
									v6.health_text.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
									v6.health_text.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
									v6.health_text.Position = UDim2.new(1, 3, 1 - lastHealth, 0)
								end
							else
								v6.health_text.Visible = false
							end
						else
							v6.health_holder.Visible = false
							v6.health_text.Visible = false
						end

						if v5.esp_names then
							v6.name_label.Visible = true
							v6.name_label.Text = k.Name
							v6.name_label.TextColor3 = v5.names_color
						else
							v6.name_label.Visible = false
						end

						if v5.esp_distance then
							v6.distance_label.Visible = true
							v6.distance_label.Text = string.format("%dm", math.round((position - head.Position).Magnitude))
							v6.distance_label.TextColor3 = v5.distance_color
						else
							v6.distance_label.Visible = false
						end

						if v5.esp_tool then
							local name = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
							name = name and name.Name or v5.tool_no_tool_text

							if name ~= v6.last_tool then
								v6.last_tool = name
								v6.tool_label.Text = name
							end

							v6.tool_label.Visible = true
							v6.tool_label.TextColor3 = v5.tools_color
						else
							v6.tool_label.Visible = false
						end

						if v5.esp_flags then
							v6.flags.Visible = true
							v6.flags.Position = UDim2.new(1, v5.flags_offset, 0, 0)
							local activeFlags = v6.active_flags
							table.clear(activeFlags)
							local match = tostring(humanoid:GetState()):match("HumanoidStateType%.(.+)$")

							if match and match ~= "Running" and match ~= "None" then
								activeFlags[#activeFlags + 1] = { text = match, color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 80) }
							end

							local assemblyLinearVelocity = humanoidRootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
							local v9 = math.round(math.sqrt(assemblyLinearVelocity.X * assemblyLinearVelocity.X + assemblyLinearVelocity.Z * assemblyLinearVelocity.Z))

							if v9 > 24 then
								activeFlags[#activeFlags + 1] = { text = v9 .. " spd", color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100) }
							end

							if k:GetAttribute("Cheating") then
								activeFlags[#activeFlags + 1] = { text = "Exploiting", color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60) }
							end

							for k2, v10 in activeFlags, nil, nil do
								local v11 = v6.cached_flags[k2]

								if not v11 then
									v11 = fn50(v6.flags, 10)
									v11.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
									v11.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, textSize + 2)
									v6.cached_flags[k2] = v11
								end

								v11.Text = v10.text
								v11.TextColor3 = v10.color
								v11.Visible = true
							end

							for i = #activeFlags + 1, #v6.cached_flags do
								v6.cached_flags[i].Visible = false
							end
						else
							v6.flags.Visible = false
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

do
	local v5 = tbl11.Visuals:AddLeftGroupbox("self visuals")

	getgenv().particle_aura = getgenv().particle_aura or {
		enabled = false,
		particle = "angel",
		color = Color3.fromRGB(133, 220, 255),
		transparency = 0.2,
		active = {},
		char_added_connection = nil,
		dropdown = nil,
	}

	getgenv().particle_aura_sources = getgenv().particle_aura_sources or {
		starlight = "rbxassetid://134645216613107",
		heavenly = "rbxassetid://139300897520961",
		ribbon = "rbxassetid://132069507632161",
		sakura = "rbxassetid://81755778619404",
		angel = "rbxassetid://97658130917593",
		wind = "rbxassetid://80694081850877",
		flow = "rbxassetid://119913533725648",
		star = "rbxassetid://73754563740680",
	}

	getgenv().particle_aura_clear = function()
		for i = 1, #getgenv().particle_aura.active do
			local v6 = getgenv().particle_aura.active[i]

			if v6 then
				pcall(function()
					v6:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			getgenv().particle_aura.active[i] = nil
		end
	end

	getgenv().particle_aura_tint = function(arg)
		if not arg then
			return
		end
		local color = getgenv().particle_aura.color
		local transparency = getgenv().particle_aura.transparency or 0.2
		local className = arg.ClassName

		if className == "PointLight" or className == "Smoke" or className == "Fire" then
			arg.Color = color

			if className == "Fire" then
				arg.SecondaryColor = color:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.25)
			end
		elseif className == "Sparkles" then
			arg.SparkleColor = color
		elseif arg:IsA("Light") then
			arg.Color = color
		elseif className == "ParticleEmitter" or className == "Beam" or className == "Trail" then
			local new = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new
			arg.Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color), new(1, color) })

			pcall(function()
				local new2 = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new
				arg.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, transparency), new2(1, transparency))
			end)
		end
	end

	getgenv().particle_aura_load_model = function()
		local particle = getgenv().particle_aura.particle
		local v6 = getgenv().particle_aura_sources[particle]
		if not v6 then
			return nil
		end
		local objects = nil
		if pcall(function()
			objects = game:GetObjects(v6)
		end) and objects and objects[1] then
			return objects[1]
		end
		return nil
	end

	getgenv().particle_aura_apply = function()
		getgenv().particle_aura_clear()
		if not getgenv().particle_aura.enabled then
			return
		end
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local v6 = getgenv().particle_aura_load_model()
		if not v6 then
			return
		end
		local v7 = next
		local children, v8 = v6:GetChildren()

		for _, v9 in v7, children, v8 do
			if v9:IsA("BasePart") then
				local v10 = character:FindFirstChild(v9.Name, true)

				if v10 and v10:IsA("BasePart") then
					local v11 = next
					local children2, v12 = v9:GetChildren()

					for _, v13 in v11, children2, v12 do
						local clone = v13:Clone()
						clone.Name = "\0\0"
						clone.Parent = v10
						getgenv().particle_aura_tint(clone)
						local v14 = next
						local descendants, v15 = clone:GetDescendants()

						for _, v16 in v14, descendants, v15 do
							getgenv().particle_aura_tint(v16)
						end

						table.insert(getgenv().particle_aura.active, clone)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		pcall(function()
			v6:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	v5:AddToggle("particle_aura_enabled", {
		Text = "particle aura",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(enabled)
			getgenv().particle_aura.enabled = enabled
			getgenv().particle_aura_apply()
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("particle_aura_color", {
		Text = "aura color",
		Default = Color3.fromRGB(133, 220, 255),
		Callback = function(color)
			getgenv().particle_aura.color = color

			if getgenv().particle_aura.enabled then
				for _, v6 in next, getgenv().particle_aura.active, nil do
					getgenv().particle_aura_tint(v6)
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	local addDropdown = v5.AddDropdown

	local tbl15 = {
		Text = "aura type",
		Default = "angel",
		Values = { "starlight", "heavenly", "sakura", "angel", "wind", "star" },
		Callback = function(particle)
			getgenv().particle_aura.particle = particle

			if getgenv().particle_aura.enabled then
				getgenv().particle_aura_apply()
			end
		end,
	}

	getgenv().particle_aura.dropdown = addDropdown(v5, "particle_aura_type", tbl15)

	if getgenv().particle_aura.char_added_connection then
		getgenv().particle_aura.char_added_connection:Disconnect()
	end

	local characterAdded = localPlayer3.CharacterAdded

	getgenv().particle_aura.char_added_connection = characterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(0.35)

		if getgenv().particle_aura.enabled then
			getgenv().particle_aura_apply()
		end
	end)

	if getgenv().particle_aura.enabled and localPlayer3.Character then
		task.spawn(function()
			getgenv().particle_aura_apply()
		end)
	end

	getgenv().self_fx = getgenv().self_fx or {
		self_chams_enabled = false,
		forcefield_color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		rainbow_forcefield = false,
		semi_transparent = false,
		original_part_material = {},
		original_part_color = {},
		original_part_transparency = {},
		rainbow_connection = nil,
		character_added_connection = nil,
	}

	getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams = function()
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local v6 = next
		local descendants, v7 = character:GetDescendants()

		for _, v8 in v6, descendants, v7 do
			if v8:IsA("BasePart") then
				if getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled then
					if not getgenv().self_fx.original_part_material[v8] then
						local material = v8.Material
						getgenv().self_fx.original_part_material[v8] = material
						local color = v8.Color
						getgenv().self_fx.original_part_color[v8] = color
					end

					v8.Material = Enum.Material.ForceField
					v8.Color = getgenv().self_fx.forcefield_color
				else
					local v9 = getgenv().self_fx.original_part_material[v8]
					local v10 = getgenv().self_fx.original_part_color[v8]

					if v9 then
						v8.Material = v9
					end

					if v10 then
						v8.Color = v10
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if not getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled then
			getgenv().self_fx.original_part_material = {}
			getgenv().self_fx.original_part_color = {}
		end
	end

	getgenv().self_fx_apply_semi_transparent = function()
		local character = localPlayer3.Character
		if not character then
			return
		end
		local v6 = next
		local descendants, v7 = character:GetDescendants()

		for _, v8 in v6, descendants, v7 do
			if v8:IsA("BasePart") then
				if getgenv().self_fx.semi_transparent then
					if getgenv().self_fx.original_part_transparency[v8] == nil then
						local transparency = v8.Transparency
						getgenv().self_fx.original_part_transparency[v8] = transparency
					end

					v8.Transparency = 0.5
				else
					local v9 = getgenv().self_fx.original_part_transparency[v8]

					if v9 ~= nil then
						v8.Transparency = v9
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if not getgenv().self_fx.semi_transparent then
			getgenv().self_fx.original_part_transparency = {}
		end
	end

	getgenv().self_fx_set_rainbow_forcefield = function(rainbowForcefield)
		getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_forcefield = rainbowForcefield

		if getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_connection then
			getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_connection:Disconnect()
			getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_connection = nil
		end

		if not rainbowForcefield then
			return
		end
		local renderStepped = RunService.RenderStepped

		getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_connection = renderStepped:Connect(function()
			local n = v2() % 5 / 5
			getgenv().self_fx.forcefield_color = Color3.fromHSV(n, 1, 1)

			if getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled then
				getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams()
			end
		end)
	end

	getgenv().self_fx_refresh_all = function()
		getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams()
		getgenv().self_fx_apply_semi_transparent()
	end

	if getgenv().self_fx.character_added_connection then
		getgenv().self_fx.character_added_connection:Disconnect()
	end

	local characterAdded2 = localPlayer3.CharacterAdded

	getgenv().self_fx.character_added_connection = characterAdded2:Connect(function()
		getgenv().self_fx.original_part_material = {}
		getgenv().self_fx.original_part_color = {}
		getgenv().self_fx.original_part_transparency = {}
		task.delay(0.35, getgenv().self_fx_refresh_all)
	end)

	v5:AddToggle("self_chams_enabled", {
		Text = "self forcefield",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(selfChamsEnabled)
			getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled = selfChamsEnabled
			getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams()
		end,
	}):AddColorPicker("self_chams_color", {
		Text = "forcefield color",
		Default = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		Callback = function(forcefieldColor)
			getgenv().self_fx.forcefield_color = forcefieldColor

			if getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled and not getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_forcefield then
				getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams()
			end
		end,
	})

	v5:AddToggle("self_rainbow_forcefield", {
		Text = "rainbow forcefield",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(arg)
			getgenv().self_fx_set_rainbow_forcefield(arg)

			if arg then
				if getgenv().self_fx.self_chams_enabled then
					getgenv().self_fx_apply_self_chams()
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	v5:AddToggle("self_semi_transparent", {
		Text = "semi transparent",
		Default = false,
		Callback = function(semiTransparent)
			getgenv().self_fx.semi_transparent = semiTransparent
			getgenv().self_fx_apply_semi_transparent()
		end,
	})
end

if localPlayer3.Character then
	task.spawn(function()
		getgenv().self_fx_refresh_all()

		if getgenv().self_fx.rainbow_forcefield then
			getgenv().self_fx_set_rainbow_forcefield(true)
		end
	end)
end

	local tbl15 = {

	local function fn50(arg, arg2)
		local instance = Instance.new("Attachment")
		instance.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg)
		instance.Parent = terrain
		local instance2 = Instance.new("Attachment")
		instance2.CFrame = CFrame.new(arg2)
		instance2.Parent = terrain
		local instance3 = Instance.new("Beam")
		local laser = tbl15[Options.typeoftracersxd.Value] or tbl15["laser"]

		for k, v5 in pairs(laser) do
			instance3[k] = v5
		end

		instance3.Width0 = Options.tracers_size.Value
		instance3.Width1 = Options.tracers_size.Value
		instance3.TextureSpeed = Options.tracers_ishowspeed.Value
		instance3.Color = ColorSequence.new(Options["tracers color"].Value, Options["tracers color gradient"].Value)
		instance3.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.2, 1)
		instance3.Attachment0 = instance
		instance3.Attachment1 = instance2
		instance3.Parent = terrain

		task.delay(Options.tracers_lifetime.Value, function()
			instance3:Destroy()
			instance:Destroy()
			instance2:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	local function fn51()
		for _, v5 in pairs(getgc(true)) do

			if type(v5) == "table" and rawget(v5, "createBullet") and not rawget(v5, "_isHooked") then
				rawset(v5, "_isHooked", true)

				local v6 = luraph_runtime2(function(arg, arg2, arg3)
					if not Toggles.tracers_______enabled_.Value then
						return arg(arg2, arg3)
					end
					local position = typeof(arg2) == "Vector3" and arg2 or arg2.Muzzle and arg2.Muzzle.Position
					local character = localPlayer3.Character
					local flag3 = character and position

					if flag3 then
						flag3 = typeof(arg3) == "Vector3"
					end

					if flag3 then
						for _, child in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
							if child:IsA("Tool") and child:FindFirstChild("Muzzle") and (child.Muzzle.Position - position).Magnitude < 2 then
								fn50(position, arg3)
								return nil
							end
						end
					end

					return arg(arg2, arg3)
				end)

				local createBullet = v5.createBullet
				local createTaser = v5.createTaser
				local createSniper = v5.createSniper

				v5.createBullet = luraph_runtime2(function(arg, arg2)
					return v6(createBullet, arg, arg2)
				end)

				v5.createTaser = luraph_runtime2(function(arg, arg2)
					return v6(createTaser, arg, arg2)
				end)

				v5.createSniper = luraph_runtime2(function(arg, arg2)
					return v6(createSniper, arg, arg2)
				end)
			end
		end
	end

	local tbl16 = { "laser", "light", "flow", "swirling", "chains" }
	local v5 = tbl11.Visuals:AddRightGroupbox("bullet tracers")
	v5:AddDropdown("typeoftracersxd", { Values = tbl16, Default = 1, Text = "selected tracer" })

	fn51()

		getgenv().server_hop_list = getgenv().server_hop_pool.data
		getgenv().server_hop_page = getgenv().server_hop_pool.nextPageCursor
		local serverHopPage = getgenv().server_hop_page

		if serverHopPage then
			serverHopPage = getgenv().server_hop_page ~= ""
		end

		if serverHopPage then
			serverHopPage = getgenv().server_hop_page ~= "null"
		end

		if serverHopPage then
			local v7 = HttpService
			local jsonDecode = v7.JSONDecode
			local v8 = game
			local httpGet = v8.HttpGet
			local placeId = game.PlaceId
			getgenv().server_hop_list = jsonDecode(v7, httpGet(v8, "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor=" .. getgenv().server_hop_page)).data
		end

		getgenv().server_hop_target_id = nil
		getgenv().server_hop_min_latency = math.huge
		local v7 = tonumber
		getgenv().server_hop_limit = v7(getgenv().server_hop_list[1].maxPlayers)
		getgenv().server_hop_threshold = getgenv().server_hop_limit / 2

		for i = 1, #getgenv().server_hop_list do
			getgenv().server_hop_v = getgenv().server_hop_list[i]
			local v8 = tonumber
			getgenv().server_hop_p_count = v8(getgenv().server_hop_v.playing)
			local v9 = tonumber
			getgenv().server_hop_p_ms = v9(getgenv().server_hop_v.ping)

			if getgenv().server_hop_p_count < getgenv().server_hop_limit and getgenv().server_hop_p_count > getgenv().server_hop_threshold then
				if getgenv().server_hop_p_ms < getgenv().server_hop_min_latency then
					getgenv().server_hop_min_latency = getgenv().server_hop_p_ms
					getgenv().server_hop_target_id = getgenv().server_hop_v.id
				end
			end
		end

		if getgenv().server_hop_target_id then
			TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, getgenv().server_hop_target_id)
		else
			Library:Notify("no other servers available", 3)

		return getgenv().music_player.dir_path .. str

	getgenv().music_player.download = function(arg)
		local v6 = getgenv().music_player.get_path(arg)
		if isfile(v6) then
			return v6
		end
		local ok, result = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, arg.url, true)
		if ok and result and #result > 10000 then
			writefile(v6, result)
			return v6
		end
	end

	getgenv().music_player.play = function()
		if #getgenv().music_player.track_list == 0 then
			return Library:Notify("no tracks found", 3)
		end
		local v6 = getgenv().music_player.track_list[getgenv().music_player.state.index]

		if getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj then
			getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj:Stop()
			getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj:Destroy()
		end

		local v7 = getgenv().music_player.download(v6)
		if not v7 then
			return Library:Notify("download failed", 3)
		end
		local ok, soundId = pcall(getcustomasset, v7)
		if not ok then
			return Library:Notify("asset error", 3)
		end
		local instance = Instance.new("Sound")
		instance.SoundId = soundId
		instance.Volume = getgenv().music_player.state.vol
		instance.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")

		instance.Ended:Connect(function()
			getgenv().music_player.state.index = getgenv().music_player.state.index % #getgenv().music_player.track_list + 1
			task.wait(0.5)
			getgenv().music_player.play()
		end)

		getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj = instance
		instance:Play()
		getgenv().music_player.state.playing = true
		Library:Notify("now playing: " .. v6.name, 3)
	end

	local musicPlayerNames = {
		"sombr - Back to Friends",
		"Tame Impala - One More Hour",
		"Lil Peep - Star Shopping",
		"Trippie Redd - Wish",
		"Kate Bush - Running Up That Hill",
		"Xxx & Trippie - Fuck Love",
		"Djo - End of Beggining",
		"Miss Me",
		"Lil Peep - Nuts",
		"Somewhere Only We Know",
		"Headlock x Headlock",
		"Ken Carson - ss",
		"Scars",
		"Playboi Carti - EVIL J0RDAN",
		"Playboi Carti - Timeless",
	}

	getgenv().music_player_names = musicPlayerNames

	getgenv().music_player_group:AddDropdown("song_dropdown", {
		Values = getgenv().music_player_names,
		Default = 1,
		Text = "select song",
		Callback = function(arg)
			for i, v6 in ipairs(getgenv().music_player.track_list) do
				if v6.name == arg then
					getgenv().music_player.state.index = i
					break
				end
			end
		end,
	})

	getgenv().music_player_group:AddSlider("volume_slider", {
		Text = "volume",
		Default = 5,
		Min = 0,
		Max = 10,
		Rounding = 1,
		Compact = true,
		Callback = function(arg)
			getgenv().music_player.state.vol = arg / 10

			if getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj then
				getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj.Volume = getgenv().music_player.state.vol
			end
		end,
	})

	getgenv().music_player_group:AddButton("play", getgenv().music_player.play)

	getgenv().music_player_group:AddButton("stop", function()
		if getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj then
			getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj:Stop()
			getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj:Destroy()
			getgenv().music_player.state.active_obj = nil
		end

		getgenv().music_player.state.playing = false
		Library:Notify("music stopped", 3)
	end)

	getgenv().music_player_group:AddButton("next", function()
		getgenv().music_player.state.index = getgenv().music_player.state.index % #getgenv().music_player.track_list + 1

		if getgenv().music_player.state.playing then
			getgenv().music_player.play()
		end
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		for _, v6 in ipairs(getgenv().music_player.track_list) do
			task.spawn(getgenv().music_player.download, v6)
		end
	end)

	Library.KeybindFrame.Visible = true
	other:AddLabel("ui keybind"):AddKeyPicker("ui_keybind", { Default = "RightShift", NoUI = false, Text = "ui keybind" })
end

Library.ToggleKeybind = Options.MenuKeybind
lib:SetLibrary(Library)
lib:BuildConfigSection(tbl11["UI Settings"])
lib:LoadAutoloadConfig()
